# Review — 045-decision-state-drift-detection

## Summary

Window from `d1853444` (`base-source: reopen`, the parent of 045's `done → in-progress` in `70bb4af5`; no review since), `review-owed: true`: 10 scope entries, each a file, no pattern, with every other path the window changed excluded as another spec's commit, and one path deleted since the base, `framework/migrations/legacy-constitutions-region.md`, absent. Examined 10 of 10. The two commits that bring paths into this scope were read for what they changed: `70bb4af5`'s cut of Rationale from the rule format, in `framework/constitution.md` §rules and 045's Resolved Question, which now names Statement and Verification units; and `10302897`'s corrections for 079's retirement of the markdown-only path, in 045's `spec.md` (AC15 annotated) and `plan.md`, and in 022's `spec.md` and `data-model.md`. 045's plan now says `check-artifacts`' compiled description names its families and 067's data model records them with their tiers, which `specs/067-analyze-and-audit-checks/data-model.md` lines 46-47 do. 022's lines saying a host runs `ductus <name>` between acquisition and the restart describe `/ductus`'s own State-B window, where the bootstrap reaches its primitives through the CLI; `bbff7a53` corrected 022's signpost to say a command stops without the server and left them, as this run does. `framework/constitution.md` was read whole this session at `3e7b3828`, unchanged since. The five shared paths, `AGENTS.md`, the three runtime workflows and `runtime/Cargo.lock`, were read by 015's review in this session from `5bbc1887`, an older base than this window's, and are counted on that reading (SEC-DEPS-001, BE-DEPS-001, BE-DEPS-002). Of the 12 rule files, the passes applied SEC-DEPS-001, the BE-DEPS, QUAL and CFG rules: no other rule's subject is in these files. No finding and no observation; no stored waiver or decision. No fix, so the fix round from `start-fix-rounds`' base `19dfa85f` is empty.
